break-words"><time class="block mb-2 md:mb-0 md:inline-block article-published-date" dateTime="2026-05-22T00:00:00.000Z">22 May 2026</time><span class="hidden md:inline-block md:mx-2">•</span><a class="hover:text-blue-400 text-xs md:text-sm whitespace-nowrap author-name" href="/security-labs/author/salim-bitam">Salim Bitam</a></div><h1 class="font-bold leading-tighter text-3xl md:text-5xl"><span>PHANTOMPULSE: anatomy of a hijackable blockchain-C2&nbsp;RAT</span></h1><p class="text-zinc-200 text-base md:text-xl">Elastic Security Labs presents a detailed reverse-engineering analysis of PHANTOMPULSE, the long-lived RAT delivered to crypto-sector victims through the REF6598 intrusion set.</p><div class="flex items-center mt-4 text-zinc-200 text-sm space-x-4 border-t border-white/25 pt-4"><span class="flex items-center space-x-1"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke-width="1.5" stroke="currentColor" aria-hidden="true" class="h-4 w-4 text-zinc-400"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M12 6v6h4.5m4.5 0a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z"></path></svg><span>18 min read</span></span><span class="flex items-center space-x-1"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke-width="1.5" stroke="currentColor" aria-hidden="true" class="h-4 w-4 text-zinc-400"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M9.568 3H5.25A2.25 2.25 0 003 5.25v4.318c0 .597.237 1.17.659 1.591l9.581 9.581c.699.699 1.78.872 2.607.33a18.095 18.095 0 005.223-5.223c.542-.827.369-1.908-.33-2.607L11.16 3.66A2.25 2.25 0 009.568 3z"></path><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M6 6h.008v.008H6V6z"></path></svg><span><a class="hover:text-blue-400 whitespace-nowrap" href="/security-labs/category/malware-analysis">Malware Analysis</a>, </span><span><a class="hover:text-blue-400 whitespace-nowrap" href="/security-labs/category/threat-intelligence">Threat Intelligence</a></span></span></div></div><div class="lg:max-w-7xl mx-auto relative mt-12 lg:grid lg:grid-cols-4 lg:gap-8 items-start"><div class="flex lg:col-span-3"><div class="prose lg:prose-lg prose-invert w-full article-content"><div><p>Elastic Security Labs&#x27;s prior coverage of <a href="https://www.elastic.co/security-labs/phantom-in-the-vault">REF6598</a> documented an intrusion set whose Windows toolchain landed via Obsidian plugin abuse, escalated via an in-memory PE loader (PHANTOMPULL), and finished with a RAT (PHANTOMPULSE). That post focused on delivery. This post analyzes the final stage: PHANTOMPULSE, an implant that ships three process-injection techniques, resolves its C2 through Ethereum/Base/Optimism transaction inputs, and bypasses UAC via the public <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">schuac</code> technique. The analysis surfaces a sinkhole-able blockchain C2 channel, a unified hardware-breakpoint primitive that disables AMSI / WLDP / ETW and pervasive AI-assisted-development fingerprints in the implant&#x27;s debug strings.</p>
<h2 class="font-bold text-2xl md:text-4xl relative"><span id="key-takeaways" class="absolute -top-32"></span>Key takeaways</h2>
<ul>
<li>PHANTOMPULSE implements three injection techniques adapted from recent public offensive-security PoCs.</li>
<li>AMSI, WLDP, and ETW are bypassed via a single shared HWBP primitive</li>
<li>The blockchain C2 resolver has <strong>no sender verification</strong>, allowing a defender to override the C2 URL for every implant by posting a single transaction</li>
<li>Strong AI-assisted-development indicators present in the binary</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="font-bold text-2xl md:text-4xl relative"><span id="a-note-on-ai-assisted-development" class="absolute -top-32"></span>A note on AI-assisted development</h2>
<p>PHANTOMPULSE bears strong fingerprints of AI coding assistance, visible throughout the debug strings.</p>
<p>The clearest tells:</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Structured step numbering</strong> in operational logs: <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">[STEP 1] Staged mode — payload downloaded from C2 at runtime</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">[STEP 1/3] Scheduled Task (DotNetSvcUpdateTask, logon + every 3 min)</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">[STEP 2/3] Boot Task (DotNetSvcCoreTask, INTERACTIVE_TOKEN + BootTrigger)</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">[UNINSTALL 4/6] Removing persist_loader DLL + registry PE data...</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">[REPAIR] Reinstalling boot task (INTERACTIVE_TOKEN)...</code>.</li>
<li><strong>ENTER/DONE function tracing</strong>: <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">&quot;[HEIS] encrypt_text_only ENTER&quot;</code> / <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">&quot;[HEIS] encrypt_text_only DONE&quot;</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">&quot;KeylogResolveAPIs: ENTER&quot;</code>. The diagnostic style LLMs default to when generating new functions.</li>
<li><strong>Verbose diagnostics</strong>: <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">&quot;FindHostProcessEx: scan stats: total=%lu sessSkip=%lu openFail=%lu native=%lu wow64=%lu mapReject=%lu dbgReject=%lu sess=%lu&quot;</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">&quot;ManualMap: thread hijacked and resumed — DLL injection via thread hijack complete&quot;</code>. Self-explanatory output, unusually talkative for malware.</li>
<li><strong>Em dashes in C strings</strong>: <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">&quot;elevate: FAIL — no deployed DLL path&quot;</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">&quot;&gt;&gt;&gt; .elevate: NOT proxy — spawning trusted host to handle elevation&quot;</code>.</li>
</ul>
<p></p>
<h2 class="font-bold text-2xl md:text-4xl relative"><span id="execution-chain" class="absolute -top-32"></span>Execution chain</h2>
<p><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">MainEntryLogic</code> is the orchestration function that runs the full initialization sequence before entering the C2 loop:</p>
<pre><code>start
 └─ WinMain
     └─ MainEntryLogic
         1. DynInit                // Bootstrap API resolution
         2. ElevationStateCheck    // &quot;.elevate&quot; marker detection, routes by token elevation state
         3. SingleInstanceCheck    // XOR-decrypted mutex, exit if already running
         4. EvasionInit            // Direct syscalls + ETW HWBP
         5. SyscallResolverInit    // CPUID + hash-based kernel32 resolution
         6. SleepMaskInit          // Sleep obfuscation setup
         7. ComputeMachineID       // DJB2(module name) ^ volume serial
         8. IsRunningHollowed      // Process hollowing self-check
         9. CollectSysInfo         // CPU, GPU, RAM, OS, AV, apps
        10. FilelessPersist        // Drop stub DLL, registry artifact
        11. InstallPersistence     // Three scheduled tasks via COM ITaskService
        12. C2Loop_Init → C2Loop_Main</code></pre>
<p>At startup, the implant DJB2-hashes the user name and computer name and looks each up in a precomputed table. A match exits the process. Brute-forcing the table against public anti-sandbox wordlists recovered 20 of the 61 entries: <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">WDAGUtilityAccount</code> (Windows Defender Application Guard), several <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">DESKTOP-XXXXXXX</code> default-VM names, and the <a href="https://gist.github.com/0x-Apollyon/04b30400b51136a19c739a8ec4dc95d5">Joe Sandbox personas</a> (<code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">abby</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">patex</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">george</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">john</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">lisa</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">frank</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">RDhJ0CNFevzX</code>).</p>
<h2 class="font-bold text-2xl md:text-4xl relative"><span id="defense-evasion" class="absolute -top-32"></span>Defense evasion</h2>
<h3 class="font-bold leading-tight text-xl md:text-3xl relative"><span id="direct-syscalls-and-api-wrappers" class="absolute -top-32"></span>Direct syscalls and API wrappers</h3>
<p>PHANTOMPULSE resolves ntdll functions by walking <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">PEB→Ldr</code> with DJB2 hashes, extracts System Service Numbers (SSNs) from each NT function&#x27;s prologue, and builds private syscall stubs. These stubs are wrapped in higher-level helpers used throughout the rest of the implant:</p>
<ul>
<li><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">NtCreateFile_Wrap</code></li>
<li><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">NtWriteFile_Wrap</code></li>
<li><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">NtClose_Wrap</code></li>
<li><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">NtCreateSection_Wrap</code></li>
<li><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">NtMapViewOfSection_Wrap</code></li>
<li><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">NtProtectVirtualMemory_Wrap</code></li>
<li><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">NtWriteVirtualMemory_Wrap</code></li>
</ul>
<p>The rest of the implant calls these wrappers instead of <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">kernel32</code>/<code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">ntdll</code> exports, defeating user-mode <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">ntdll</code> hooks (IAT replacements, inline detours, or trampoline patches) that EDR products inject into the documented API surface.</p>
<p>A single helper function routes every disk write through <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">NtCreateFile</code> + <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">NtWriteFile</code> directly, with delete-and-retry on access errors.</p>
<h3 class="font-bold leading-tight text-xl md:text-3xl relative"><span id="string-and-config-obfuscation" class="absolute -top-32"></span>String and config obfuscation</h3>
<p>PHANTOMPULSE uses four XOR layers for different artifacts:</p>
<div class="table-container"><table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;word-wrap:break-word"><thead><tr><th>What</th><th>Key</th><th>Where the key lives</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>C2 fallback URL, mutex, drop-path filenames</td><td>16-byte: <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">F7 7C 8E 40 DF C1 7B E5 E7 4D 86 79 D5 B3 53 41</code></td><td>Embedded in <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">.rdata</code></td></tr><tr><td>Blockchain provider hostnames (UTF-16 LE)</td><td>8-byte: <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">5A 3C 7E 1D 9F 2B 4E 8A</code></td><td>Embedded in <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">.rdata</code></td></tr><tr><td>COM Elevation Moniker, keylog file payload</td><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">0xE95CA237</code>, computed at runtime to keep the constant out of <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">.rdata</code></td><td>Computed, not stored</td></tr><tr><td>C2 URL pulled from blockchain transaction <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">input</code></td><td>The resolver wallet address itself</td><td>Reused from the public lookup key</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p></p>
<h3 class="font-bold leading-tight text-xl md:text-3xl relative"><span id="amsi-wldp-and-etw-bypass-via-hardware-breakpoints" class="absolute -top-32"></span>AMSI, WLDP, and ETW bypass via hardware breakpoints</h3>
<p>PHANTOMPULSE disables AMSI, the Windows Lockdown Policy code-trust check, and ETW telemetry through a single shared primitive: a hardware breakpoint planted on each API entry, intercepted by a vectored exception handler that fakes the return value without inline patching.</p>
<div class="table-container"><table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;word-wrap:break-word"><thead><tr><th>Slot</th><th>Target API</th><th>Spoofed return (RAX)</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>DR0</td><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">WldpQueryDynamicCodeTrust</code></td><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">0</code> (<code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">S_OK</code>)</td></tr><tr><td>DR1</td><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">AmsiScanBuffer</code></td><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">0x80070057</code> (<code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">E_INVALIDARG</code>)</td></tr><tr><td>DR2</td><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">EtwEventWrite</code></td><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">0</code> (<code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">STATUS_SUCCESS</code>)</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p></p>
<p>The mechanism, step by step:</p>
<ol>
<li>The implant resolves the target API. AMSI and WLDP go through <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">LoadLibraryA</code> + hash-based export lookup; ETW uses a PEB→Ldr walk since ntdll is already loaded.</li>
<li>The HWBP descriptor (target API address, mode, spoofed return value) is written into one of four 40-byte slots in a global slot table.</li>
<li>A helper thread suspends the target thread, calls <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">NtGetContextThread</code> / <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">NtSetContextThread</code> to write DR0–DR3 + DR7, then resumes. (If the implant&#x27;s vectored exception handler is already installed, an in-process <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">STATUS_BREAKPOINT</code> is raised instead, letting the VEH read the slot table and program the DRs without a helper thread.)</li>
<li>When the protected API is called, the CPU raises <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">Debug Exception</code> on the function&#x27;s first instruction.</li>
<li>The implant&#x27;s vectored exception handler intercepts the <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">Debug Exception</code>, walks its 4-slot table to find the firing address, and modifies the thread context: <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">CONTEXT.Rax</code> is set to the per-slot spoofed return value, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">CONTEXT.Rip</code> is redirected to a pre-stored &quot;skip&quot; thunk that returns to the caller.</li>
<li>The handler returns <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">EXCEPTION_CONTINUE_EXECUTION</code>. The caller sees the spoofed RAX as if the API had run.</li>
</ol>
<p></p>
<p>The dispatcher serves two paths. One handler (<code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">VEH_Dispatcher</code>) processes both the implant&#x27;s own <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">RaiseException(STATUS_BREAKPOINT)</code> calls (used to seed and re-program the DR registers from the slot table) and the <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">STATUS_SINGLE_STEP</code> exceptions that fire when a protected API is called. The exception code drives the branch: <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">STATUS_BREAKPOINT</code> triggers DR programming, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">STATUS_SINGLE_STEP</code> triggers the spoof.</p>
<p>The handler is also not registered directly. <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">AddVectoredExceptionHandler</code> receives a tiny JMP thunk allocated at runtime in a fresh <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">MEM_PRIVATE</code> page (<code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">VirtualAlloc</code> + <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">VirtualProtect</code> to <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">PAGE_EXECUTE_READ</code>). The thunk is a <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">JMP [RIP-relative]</code> indirect jump (6-byte opcode <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">FF 25 00 00 00 00</code>) followed inline by the dispatcher&#x27;s address. Because no bytes are ever written to <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">AmsiScanBuffer</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">WldpQueryDynamicCodeTrust</code>, or <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">EtwEventWrite</code>, signature-based detection that scans for prologue patches misses this entirely.</p>
<h3 class="font-bold leading-tight text-xl md:text-3xl relative"><span id="build-variant-active-and-dormant-subsystems" class="absolute -top-32"></span>Build variant: active and dormant subsystems</h3>
<p>Several subsystems exist in the binary as code or strings, but are not active in this build. This is a stripped-down build of a larger codebase.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>NTDLL unhooking</strong>: Debug strings for an unhooking subsystem live in <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">.rdata</code> (<code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">UnhookNtdll: ntdll base = %p</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">applied %d relocation fixups to .text</code>), but nothing references them. Dead in this variant.</li>
<li><strong>Registry-resident PE blob loader</strong>: earlier builds stored the next-stage PE inside the registry. This build does not, but the uninstall routine still cleans up the legacy registry blob.</li>
<li><strong>COM hijack persistence</strong>: never installed by this build. Cleanup logic for it remains in the uninstall routine.</li>
<li><strong>Print monitor persistence</strong>: same pattern as COM hijack; install path absent, uninstall path retained.</li>
</ul>
<p>The last three (registry blob loader, COM hijack, print monitor) show the opposite pattern: cleanup logic with no install logic, retained for backward compatibility against older deployments.</p>
<div class="table-container"><table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;word-wrap:break-word"><thead><tr><th>Feature</th><th>payloads build</th><th>Evidence</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Direct syscalls (SSN extraction)</td><td>Active</td><td>SSN extraction + stub generation confirmed</td></tr><tr><td>AMSI / WLDP / ETW HWBP bypass</td><td>Active</td><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">DR0</code>/<code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">DR1</code>/<code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">DR2</code> via shared helper-thread primitive</td></tr><tr><td>Three-way process injection</td><td>Active</td><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">PhantomInject</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">DbgNexum</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">ManualMap</code> are all functional</td></tr><tr><td>Blockchain C2 resolution</td><td>Active</td><td>Three Blockscout providers queried</td></tr><tr><td>NTDLL unhooking</td><td><strong>Dead code</strong></td><td>Strings present, zero code references</td></tr><tr><td>HEIS encryption</td><td><strong>Disabled</strong></td><td>Code encrypt/decrypt stubbed</td></tr><tr><td>Registry-resident PE blob loader</td><td><strong>Legacy only</strong></td><td>Only cleaned during uninstall</td></tr><tr><td>COM hijack persistence</td><td>Legacy only</td><td>Cleaned during uninstall, never installed</td></tr><tr><td>Print monitor persistence</td><td>Legacy only</td><td>Cleaned during uninstall, never installed</td></tr><tr><td>Decoy strings</td><td>Active</td><td>4 unreferenced decoy strings in <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">.rdata</code></td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<h2 class="font-bold text-2xl md:text-4xl relative"><span id="command-and-control" class="absolute -top-32"></span>Command and control</h2>
<h3 class="font-bold leading-tight text-xl md:text-3xl relative"><span id="blockchain-c2-resolution" class="absolute -top-32"></span>Blockchain C2 resolution</h3>
<p>PHANTOMPULSE decentralizes C2 lookup through three Blockscout providers:</p>
<ul>
<li><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">eth.blockscout[.]com</code> (Ethereum L1)</li>
<li><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">base.blockscout[.]com</code> (Base L2)</li>
<li><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">optimism.blockscout[.]com</code> (Optimism L2)</li>
</ul>
<p>The wallet address <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">0xc117688c530b660e15085bF3A2B664117d8672aA</code> is XOR-decrypted from storage with a 16-byte key. For each provider, the implant issues an HTTPS GET (port 443, SSL cert errors ignored), pulls the <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">input</code> field of the latest transaction, hex-decodes it, XOR-decrypts with the wallet address bytes as the key, and validates that the result begins with <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">http</code>. On total failure, it falls back to the hardcoded URL <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">https://panel.fefea22134[.]net</code>.</p>
<p></p>
<p>The resolver does not verify the sender of the transaction. It only checks that the latest decoded <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">input</code> starts with <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">http</code>. Anyone can submit a transaction to that wallet with their own URL XOR-encoded under the wallet bytes, and every PHANTOMPULSE instance of that campaign that polls afterward resolves to that URL. For network defenders, this is a viable sinkhole at the cost of one transaction.</p>
<p></p>
<h3 class="font-bold leading-tight text-xl md:text-3xl relative"><span id="endpoints-and-heartbeat" class="absolute -top-32"></span>Endpoints and heartbeat</h3>
<p>Five API paths are constructed at runtime, re-encrypted in memory with a per-session key:</p>
<div class="table-container"><table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;word-wrap:break-word"><thead><tr><th>Path</th><th>Method</th><th>Content-Type</th><th>Purpose</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">/v1/telemetry/report</code></td><td>POST</td><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">application/json</code></td><td>Heartbeat with full system telemetry</td></tr><tr><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">/v1/telemetry/tasks/&lt;machine_id&gt;</code></td><td>GET</td><td></td><td>Command fetch</td></tr><tr><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">/v1/telemetry/upload/</code></td><td>POST</td><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">image/bmp</code></td><td>Screenshot / file upload</td></tr><tr><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">/v1/telemetry/result</code></td><td>POST</td><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">application/json</code></td><td>Command result delivery</td></tr><tr><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">/v1/telemetry/keylog/</code></td><td>POST</td><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">text/plain</code></td><td>Keylog data upload</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p>The heartbeat sends a full system profile as JSON:</p>
<pre><code>{
  &quot;machine_id&quot;: &quot;&lt;uint32&gt;&quot;,
  &quot;status&quot;: &quot;online&quot;,
  &quot;cpu&quot;: &quot;&lt;model&gt;&quot;,
  &quot;gpu&quot;: &quot;&lt;description&gt;&quot;,
  &quot;ram_mb&quot;: &quot;&lt;uint32&gt;&quot;,
  &quot;os&quot;: &quot;&lt;version&gt;&quot;,
  &quot;username&quot;: &quot;&lt;user&gt;&quot;,
  &quot;computer_name&quot;: &quot;&lt;name&gt;&quot;,
  &quot;cores&quot;: &quot;&lt;uint32&gt;&quot;,
  &quot;screen_w&quot;: &quot;&lt;int&gt;&quot;,
  &quot;screen_h&quot;: &quot;&lt;int&gt;&quot;,
  &quot;privilege&quot;: &quot;&lt;user|admin|admin_nouac|system&gt;&quot;,
  &quot;build&quot;: &quot;payloads&quot;,
  &quot;public_ip&quot;: &quot;&lt;ip&gt;&quot;,
  &quot;av_list&quot;: [&quot;&lt;av1&gt;&quot;, &quot;&lt;av2&gt;&quot;],
  &quot;apps&quot;: [&quot;&lt;app1&gt;&quot;, &quot;&lt;app2&gt;&quot;],
  &quot;last_cmd&quot;: &quot;&lt;cmd&gt;&quot;,
  &quot;last_cmd_result&quot;: &quot;&lt;result&gt;&quot;
}</code></pre>
<p></p>
<p>Two response fields are parsed: <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">&quot;status&quot;:&quot;deleted&quot;</code> triggers full uninstall; <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">&quot;ip&quot;:&quot;&lt;value&gt;&quot;</code> populates the public-IP cache, but only if local discovery (ipif[.]org / icanhazip[.]com/ checkip.amazonaws[.]com) hasn&#x27;t already filled it.</p>
<p></p>
<h3 class="font-bold leading-tight text-xl md:text-3xl relative"><span id="loop-cadence-and-resilience" class="absolute -top-32"></span>Loop cadence and resilience</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Sleep</strong>: uniform random in [20, 40] seconds</li>
<li><strong>Self-healing</strong>: runs at iteration 2, then every 10th iteration</li>
<li><strong>Health-monitor tick</strong>: first call after the implant comes online, then every 5th iteration thereafter. Populates a local system-info struct (CPU%, RAM, OS version, uptime, computer name).</li>
<li><strong>Failure threshold</strong>: 10 consecutive heartbeat failures trigger a self-restart for stuck SSL/TLS recovery</li>
<li><strong>Re-resolution</strong>: on failure, blockchain re-resolution runs; if the resolved URL changes, the failure counter resets</li>
<li><strong>Public IP</strong>: <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">api4.ipify[.]org</code> → <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">ipv4.icanhazip[.]com</code> → <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">checkip.amazonaws[.]com</code></li>
<li><strong>Connectivity check</strong>: probes <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">microsoft[.]com</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">google[.]com</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">cloudflare[.]com</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">github[.]com</code></li>
</ul>
<h2 class="font-bold text-2xl md:text-4xl relative"><span id="command-dispatch" class="absolute -top-32"></span>Command dispatch</h2>
<p>The command dispatcher routes commands by DJB2 hash. Eight commands total:</p>
<p></p>
<div class="table-container"><table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;word-wrap:break-word"><thead><tr><th>Hash</th><th>Command</th><th>Behavior</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">0x04CF1142</code></td><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">inject</code></td><td>Inject shellcode/DLL/EXE. Routes by type: shellcode → <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">PhantomInject</code>; DLL → <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">ManualMap</code>; EXE → <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">DbgNexum</code>. The AMSI and WLDP HWBP bypasses are installed on the first <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">inject</code> call (the ETW HWBP is already in place from <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">EvasionInit</code>).</td></tr><tr><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">0x7C95D91A</code></td><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">drop</code></td><td>Drop the file to the disk and execute. Supports DLL, EXE, shellcode (APC injection), and MSI payloads.</td></tr><tr><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">0x9A37F083</code></td><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">screenshot</code></td><td>GDI capture, downscale to 960px wide, upload as BMP.</td></tr><tr><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">0x08DEDEF0</code></td><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">keylog</code></td><td>Start or stop the inline keylogger.</td></tr><tr><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">0x4EE251FF</code></td><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">uninstall</code></td><td>6-step cleanup and termination.</td></tr><tr><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">0x65CCC50B</code></td><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">elevate</code></td><td>UAC bypass via the <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">schuac</code> technique (<code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">IElevatedFactoryServer::ServerCreateElevatedObject(CLSID_TaskScheduler)</code>); registers a transient elevated task that relaunches the implant.</td></tr><tr><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">0xB3B5B880</code></td><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">downgrade</code></td><td>SYSTEM → elevated admin transition.</td></tr><tr><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">0x20CE3BC8</code></td><td>(self-restart)</td><td>Cascading self-terminate: <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">NtTerminateProcess(-1, 0)</code> direct syscall first; if that fails to resolve, falls back to <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">ExitProcess(0)</code>. Persistence relaunches the implant on the next scheduled task tick. Operationally equivalent to a soft restart.</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p>The eighth handler has no debug log naming it. It self-terminates; the scheduled task relaunches the implant on the next tick. The lack of LLM-style scaffolding (debug strings) makes this one of the few handlers in the binary that appears to be added by the human author rather than LLM-generated.</p>
<h2 class="font-bold text-2xl md:text-4xl relative"><span id="injection-techniques" class="absolute -top-32"></span>Injection techniques</h2>
<p>PHANTOMPULSE ships three injection techniques, one per payload type. The <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">inject</code> C2 command routes shellcode to <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">PhantomInject</code>, DLLs to <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">ManualMap</code>, and EXEs to <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">DbgNexum</code>.</p>
<p></p>
<p>The AMSI/WLDP hardware-breakpoint bypasses are installed on the first <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">inject</code> call, before any injector runs.</p>
<div class="table-container"><table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;word-wrap:break-word"><thead><tr><th>Payload type</th><th>Injector</th><th>Strategy</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Shellcode</td><td>PhantomInject</td><td>Module stomping in <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">dbghelp.dll</code> via <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">SEC_IMAGE</code></td></tr><tr><td>EXE</td><td>DbgNexum</td><td>Debug-API state machine</td></tr><tr><td>DLL</td><td>ManualMap</td><td>Full PE manual mapping</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<h3 class="font-bold leading-tight text-xl md:text-3xl relative"><span id="phantominject-module-stomping-into-dbghelpdll" class="absolute -top-32"></span>PhantomInject: module stomping into dbghelp.dll</h3>
<p>Module stomping avoids <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">MEM_PRIVATE</code> allocation by mapping a legitimate Windows DLL as <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">SEC_IMAGE</code> and overwriting <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">.text</code>:</p>
<ol>
<li>
<p>Acquires <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">SeDebugPrivilege</code> (via <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">OpenProcessToken</code> / <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">LookupPrivilegeValueW</code> / <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">AdjustTokenPrivileges</code>), then walks the process snapshot for one of seven host-process candidates (case-insensitive match). Tried in priority order: <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">sihost.exe</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">taskhostw.exe</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">backgroundTaskHost.exe</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">RuntimeBroker.exe</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">dllhost.exe</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">ctfmon.exe</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">explorer.exe</code>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Opens <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">dbghelp.dll</code> via <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">NtOpenFile</code>, creates <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">SEC_IMAGE</code> section, maps into target via <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">NtMapViewOfSection</code></p>
<p></p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Parses the local copy for <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">.text</code> RVA and size, then frees it</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Selects and suspends a thread, captures context</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Builds an <a href="https://gist.github.com/soolidsnake/f5cc038aa919db19658dfe4430a62114"><strong>82-byte save-call-restore trampoline</strong></a></p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Writes shellcode + trampoline into <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">.text</code> of the mapped DLL</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Flips protection to <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">PAGE_EXECUTE_READ</code></p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Repoints RIP to the trampoline, resumes thread</p>
</li>
</ol>
<p>To a memory scanner, the result looks like a thread executing inside legitimate <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">dbghelp.dll</code>, a file-backed image region with the right file path, section name, and first-page hash.</p>
<h3 class="font-bold leading-tight text-xl md:text-3xl relative"><span id="dbgnexum-debug-api-as-an-execution-controller" class="absolute -top-32"></span>DbgNexum: debug API as an execution controller</h3>
<p>DbgNexum handles EXE payloads. Rather than writing executable code into the target up front, it uses the Windows debug API to drive execution one exception at a time: a ROP chain whose gadgets are full Windows APIs in the target.</p>
<p>The technique is not original to PHANTOMPULSE. It is a verbatim lift of <a href="https://github.com/dis0rder0x00/DbgNexum"><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">dis0rder0x00/DbgNexum</code></a>, a public proof of concept published on GitHub on 2026-01-04. The operator kept the published technique name (<code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">&quot;DbgNexum&quot;</code>) in the implant&#x27;s debug strings unchanged, and the inner state machine is a 1:1 match: the same bait API, section name, gadget chain, and constants. PHANTOMPULSE wraps the lifted x64 core with operational scaffolding the PoC does not have: host-process selection (<code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">FindHostProcessEx</code>), a fallback that spawns a fresh <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">SysWOW64\cmd.exe</code> / <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">rundll32.exe</code> / <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">notepad.exe</code>, a custom PE-loading bootstrap (so it can carry full EXEs instead of raw shellcode), and a separate WoW64 cross-architecture variant.</p>
<p>For native x64 payloads, the implant pre-stages the PE, the bootstrap stub, and the trampoline config inside a named file-mapping section. The section name is the literal two-byte string <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">&quot;MZ&quot;</code>, the implant attaches with <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">DebugActiveProcess</code> and creates a remote thread on <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">FileTimeToSystemTime</code> with a hardware breakpoint on DR0.</p>
<p></p>
<p>When the bait hits the breakpoint, a state machine drives the target through this API chain:</p>
<ol>
<li>Redirect <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">RIP</code> to <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">DbgBreakPoint+1</code> with the trap flag set; the resulting single-step exception bridges into the rest of the chain.</li>
<li><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">LocalAlloc(LMEM_ZEROINIT, 3)</code>, allocate the 3-byte name buffer.</li>
<li><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">memcpy(buf, kernel32_base, 2)</code>, copy <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">&quot;MZ&quot;</code> from <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">kernel32.dll</code>&#x27;s DOS header into the buffer.</li>
<li><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">memset(stack+40, 0, 8)</code>: zero a stack arg slot.</li>
<li><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">OpenFileMappingA(0x1F, FALSE, &quot;MZ&quot;)</code>, open the prepared section with full section-mapping access.</li>
<li><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">MapViewOfFile(...)</code>,  map it into the target.</li>
<li>Redirect <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">RIP</code> to <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">mapped_base + 0x400</code>, the bootstrap stub. This is the only stage logged directly: <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">DbgNexumLoop64: stage 6 -&gt; stub at %llx, base=%llx</code>. (The PoC redirects to <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">mapped_base + 0</code> for raw shellcode; PHANTOMPULSE adds the <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">+0x400</code> offset to land on its custom PE loader.)</li>
</ol>
<p>Each transition intercepts the next debug event, restores <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">RSP</code>, clears the trap flag, modifies <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">RIP</code> and the argument registers (<code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">RCX</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">RDX</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">R8</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">R9</code>) for the next call, and continues the debuggee. DR0 is reused as an execute hardware breakpoint on each saved return address, so no inline patches or <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">WriteProcessMemory</code> against the target are needed. From the kernel&#x27;s view, all that happened was a thread inside <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">kernel32.dll</code> calling <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">LocalAlloc</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">OpenFileMappingA</code>, and <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">MapViewOfFile</code>.</p>
<p>The cross-architecture path (PE32 from a 64-bit implant) is a PHANTOMPULSE-only variant the public PoC does not have. It takes a shortcut: the implant walks the target&#x27;s <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">PEB.Ldr</code> via <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">NtReadVirtualMemory</code> (using <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">ProcessWow64Information</code> to pick the 32-bit or 64-bit PEB layout) to find a DLL with a callable entry point, then pre-maps a section containing a 32-bit loader stub and trampoline into both processes via <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">NtCreateSection</code> + <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">NtMapViewOfSection</code>. The redirect is just two stages: bait at <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">RtlExitUserThread</code>, then a <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">DbgBreakPoint</code>-mediated single step jumps <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">RIP</code> to the trampoline. There is no API chain on this path because the section is already mapped on both sides, so <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">OpenFileMappingA</code>/<code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">MapViewOfFile</code> aren&#x27;t needed.</p>
<p>Cross-arch host selection runs through <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">FindHostProcessEx</code>, which excludes critical system processes (<code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">csrss.exe</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">lsass.exe</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">smss.exe</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">winlogon.exe</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">services.exe</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">wininit.exe</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">svchost.exe</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">MsMpEng.exe</code>) and falls back to spawning a fresh <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">SysWOW64\cmd.exe</code> / <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">rundll32.exe</code> / <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">notepad.exe</code> if no usable WoW64 host is found.</p>
<h3 class="font-bold leading-tight text-xl md:text-3xl relative"><span id="manualmap-full-pe-mapper" class="absolute -top-32"></span>ManualMap: full PE mapper</h3>
<p>ManualMap handles DLL payloads with a complete PE manual mapping implementation:</p>
<ol>
<li>
<p>Validates MZ/PE signature; rejects PE32 in the x64 host path (debug-log: <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">&quot;PE32 DLL in x64 host is impossible&quot;</code>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Allocates <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">SizeOfImage</code> in the target via <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">NtAllocateVirtualMemory</code></p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Copies headers and sections to a local staging buffer</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Applies base relocations (<code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">IMAGE_REL_BASED_DIR64</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">IMAGE_REL_BASED_HIGHLOW</code>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Resolves imports via <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">LoadLibraryA</code> + <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">GetProcAddress</code></p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Wipes PE headers (zeros <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">SizeOfHeaders</code> bytes)</p>
<p></p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Writes the staged image into the remote allocation</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Sets per-section memory protection</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Builds a <strong>137-byte trampoline</strong> in a separate 0x2000-byte remote allocation (set to <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">PAGE_EXECUTE_READ</code>):</p>
</li>
</ol>
<p>The <a href="https://gist.github.com/soolidsnake/9f8ddf9900150c467ada95ad7838bd68">full trampoline shellcode gist</a> contains the complete bytes.</p>
<p></p>
<p>10. Hijacks a thread via suspend / get-context / set-context</p>
<h2 class="font-bold text-2xl md:text-4xl relative"><span id="privilege-escalation" class="absolute -top-32"></span>Privilege escalation</h2>
<p>The <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">elevate</code> command is a UAC bypass via the <strong><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">schuac</code> technique</strong> (<code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">IElevatedFactoryServer::ServerCreateElevatedObject(CLSID_TaskScheduler)</code>), published as <a href="https://github.com/hfiref0x/UACME/issues/129">UACME issue #129</a> by zcgonvh, <a href="https://github.com/hfiref0x/UACME/blob/6daa8d486250c20189a803838c3654aad73a541d/README.md?plain=1#L778">currently under the ID 74</a>.</p>
<p></p>
<h3 class="font-bold leading-tight text-xl md:text-3xl relative"><span id="mechanism" class="absolute -top-32"></span>Mechanism</h3>
<p><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">MaintenanceUI.dll</code>&#x27;s <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">CMaintenanceUIVirtualFactory</code> (CLSID <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">{A6BFEA43-501F-456F-A845-983D3AD7B8F0}</code>) is registered with an <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">Elevation</code> registry key, so the OS hands non-admin callers an elevated instance. Its <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">IElevatedFactoryServer</code> interface exposes <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">ServerCreateElevatedObject(rclsid, riid, ppv)</code>, which the elevated server uses to instantiate <strong>any other CLSID</strong> under its elevated context. PHANTOMPULSE feeds it <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">CLSID_TaskScheduler</code>, gets back an elevated <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">ITaskService</code>, and uses that service to register a <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">HighestAvailable</code>-RunLevel task that re-launches the implant.</p>
<h3 class="font-bold leading-tight text-xl md:text-3xl relative"><span id="the-elevate-c2-command" class="absolute -top-32"></span>The <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">elevate</code> C2 command</h3>
<p>Inside <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">ProcessCommands</code>, the elevate handler:</p>
<ol>
<li>Builds the task action. The command is <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">&lt;system_dir&gt;\rundll32.exe</code> with arguments <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">\&quot;&lt;deployed_dll&gt;\&quot;,DllRegisterServer</code>. The user identifier is <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">COMPUTERNAME\USERNAME</code> from <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">GetEnvironmentVariableW</code>. ![Elevate command call][/assets/images/blockchain-c2-phantompulse-rat-sinkhole/image17.png]</li>
<li>Writes the <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">.elevate</code> marker as a single byte (<code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">&quot;1&quot;</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">0x31</code>), not encoded parameters. The write goes through <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">NtCreateFile</code> + <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">NtWriteFile</code> to bypass user-mode hooks. The marker is just a presence flag; the elevation parameters travel inside the task definition the implant is about to register.</li>
<li>XOR-decrypts the COM Elevation Moniker at runtime, 66 bytes from <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">.rdata</code> xored against seed <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">0xE95CA237</code>, which decodes to <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">Elevation:Administrator!new:{A6BFEA43-501F-456F-A845-983D3AD7B8F0}</code>.</li>
<li>Calls <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">CoGetObject(moniker, &amp;BIND_OPTS3{dwClassContext=CLSCTX_LOCAL_SERVER}, IID_IElevatedFactoryServer, &amp;factory)</code> to get an elevated <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">IElevatedFactoryServer*</code>, then <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">factory-&gt;ServerCreateElevatedObject(CLSID_TaskScheduler, IID_ITaskService, &amp;elevatedTaskService)</code> to get an elevated <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">ITaskService*</code> that inherits the elevation.</li>
<li>Registers a transient task <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">DotNetSvcElevateTask</code> at <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">HighestAvailable</code> RunLevel with the rundll32 action above.</li>
<li>Deletes any pre-existing non-elevated persistent tasks so the old low-IL persistence cannot race the elevated relaunch.</li>
<li>Calls <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">ITaskService::Run</code> on the transient task and deletes it immediately afterward. Releases the single-instance mutex and exits the medium-IL implant.</li>
</ol>
<h3 class="font-bold leading-tight text-xl md:text-3xl relative"><span id="fallback-retry-via-proxy-rundll32" class="absolute -top-32"></span>Fallback retry via proxy rundll32</h3>
<p>If the <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">CoGetObject</code> / <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">RegisterTask</code> chain fails, a fallback path takes over. A separate startup path spawns a fresh <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">rundll32.exe &quot;&lt;deployed_dll&gt;&quot;,DllRegisterServer</code> directly via <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">CreateProcessW</code>, with retries (<code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">&quot;&gt;&gt;&gt; .elevate redirect attempt %d&quot;</code>). Inside that rundll32 the implant detects <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">isProxy=1</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">elevated=0</code> and <strong>retries the schuac sequence</strong> with three registration variants (<code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">ELEVATED+INTERACTIVE+user</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">ELEVATED+INTERACTIVE</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">INTERACTIVE</code>). On success, the elevated task fires and the elevated relaunch takes over. On exhaustion, the proxy logs <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">&quot;&gt;&gt;&gt; Phase 1: all registration methods failed, cleaning marker&quot;</code> and exits.</p>
<h3 class="font-bold leading-tight text-xl md:text-3xl relative"><span id="elevated-rundll32-relaunch" class="absolute -top-32"></span>Elevated rundll32 relaunch</h3>
<p>When the transient task fires, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">svchost.exe (Schedule)</code> launches <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">rundll32.exe &quot;&lt;deployed_dll&gt;&quot;,DllRegisterServer</code> under a fresh high-IL token. The implant&#x27;s <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">DllRegisterServer</code> export runs as the entry; at startup it sees the <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">.elevate</code> marker and a high-IL token and routes to the elevated path:</p>
<ul>
<li>Reads and <strong>deletes</strong> the <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">.elevate</code> marker.</li>
<li><strong>Reinstalls persistence under elevated context</strong>, including the boot task <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">DotNetSvcCoreTask</code> under <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">\Microsoft\Windows\NetFramework\</code>, registered with <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">INTERACTIVE_TOKEN</code> + <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">BootTrigger</code>, which requires admin to register.</li>
<li>Continues normal implant operation as a high-IL service.</li>
</ul>
<h3 class="font-bold leading-tight text-xl md:text-3xl relative"><span id="marker-state-routing" class="absolute -top-32"></span>Marker-state routing</h3>
<p>At every startup, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">MainEntryLogic</code> calls <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">GetFileAttributesW</code> on the <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">.elevate</code> path. If it returns <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">INVALID_FILE_ATTRIBUTES</code>, the implant skips all elevation logic and starts normally. If the marker exists, the implant gathers two more facts: whether the current process is a <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">rundll32.exe</code>/<code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">regsvr32.exe</code> proxy, and whether the token is elevated, then routes on the combination:</p>
<div class="table-container"><table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;word-wrap:break-word"><thead><tr><th>Marker</th><th>Proxy</th><th>Elevated</th><th>What happens</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>absent</td><td>n/a</td><td>n/a</td><td>normal startup, no special routing</td></tr><tr><td>present</td><td>no</td><td>no</td><td>spawn proxy rundll32 to retry the schuac chain, then exit</td></tr><tr><td>present</td><td>yes</td><td>no</td><td>retry schuac inside the rundll32; on exhaustion delete marker and exit</td></tr><tr><td>present</td><td>*</td><td>yes</td><td>delete marker, reinstall persistence with the boot task, continue as high-IL</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p>The marker file&#x27;s <em>contents</em> are never read, its presence alone drives the routing.</p>
<p>Elastic Security Labs&#x27; <a href="https://www.elastic.co/security-labs/exploring-windows-uac-bypasses-techniques-and-detection-strategies">Exploring Windows UAC Bypasses</a> covers detection patterns for the <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">IElevatedFactoryServer</code> class of bypass directly.</p>
<h2 class="font-bold text-2xl md:text-4xl relative"><span id="persistence" class="absolute -top-32"></span>Persistence</h2>
<h3 class="font-bold leading-tight text-xl md:text-3xl relative"><span id="three-scheduled-tasks" class="absolute -top-32"></span>Three scheduled tasks</h3>
<p>PHANTOMPULSE installs three scheduled tasks via the COM <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">ITaskService</code> interface, each executing <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">rundll32.exe &quot;&lt;stub_dll&gt;&quot;,DllRegisterServer</code>:</p>
<div class="table-container"><table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;word-wrap:break-word"><thead><tr><th>Task</th><th>Trigger</th><th>Interval</th><th>RunLevel</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">DotNetSvcUpdateTask</code></td><td>User Logon + Time</td><td>3 min</td><td>Standard</td></tr><tr><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">DotNetSvcCoreTask</code></td><td>Boot + Time</td><td>15 min</td><td>HighestAvailable + Hidden</td></tr><tr><td><code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">DotNetSvcUserTask</code></td><td>User Logon</td><td>On logon</td><td>Standard</td></tr></tbody></table></div>
<p></p>
<p>The boot task lives under <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">\Microsoft\Windows\NetFramework\</code> to blend with legitimate .NET maintenance tasks. Its full XML body is embedded in <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">.rdata</code>, not constructed at runtime. Signatures on this literal blob are durable across builds.</p>
<p>Installation tries multiple <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">RegisterTask</code> fallback variants per task, combining <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">INTERACTIVE_TOKEN</code> and <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">S4U</code> logon types with different user-identifier formats (<code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">COMPUTER\User</code>, <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">SID</code>, short username). The exact order of attempts depends on the implant&#x27;s privilege context (SYSTEM, admin, or standard user); failures fall through to the next variant until one succeeds.</p>
<h3 class="font-bold leading-tight text-xl md:text-3xl relative"><span id="stub-dll-drop" class="absolute -top-32"></span>Stub DLL drop</h3>
<p>The deployed agent lives <strong>encrypted and compressed inside the EXE&#x27;s own <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">.rdata</code></strong>. <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">FilelessPersist</code> decrypts it on demand and writes the result to disk via (<code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">NtCreateFile</code> + <code class="px-1.5 py-1 rounded not-prose bg-[var(--tw-prose-invert-pre-bg)] whitespace-break-spaces text-[85%] text-emerald-600">NtWriteFile</code>).</p>
<p>Decryption is two stages:</p>
<ol>
